===== FILE: scripts/kg/assemble_graph.py =====
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
scripts/kg/assemble_graph.py
  scripts/kg/output/<domain>/*.json  +  public/knowledge/specs.json
    -> public/knowledge/graph.json  +  docs/knowledge-index.md

Node types: rule | concept | book
Edge types: grounded_by (rule->concept, rule->book), cited_in (concept->book),
            relates_to (concept->concept)
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import re
import time
from pathlib import Path

KG_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
REPO_ROOT = KG_DIR.parents[1]
SPECS_FILE = REPO_ROOT / "public" / "knowledge" / "specs.json"
OUTPUT_DIR = KG_DIR / "output"
GRAPH_FILE = REPO_ROOT / "public" / "knowledge" / "graph.json"
INDEX_FILE = REPO_ROOT / "docs" / "knowledge-index.md"
ALIASES_FILE = KG_DIR / "aliases.json"

SEVERITY_WEIGHTS = {"critical": 4, "error": 3, "warning": 2, "info": 1}


def slug(text: str) -> str:
    return re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9]+", "_", text.lower()).strip("_")


def tokens(text: str) -> set[str]:
    return set(re.findall(r"[a-z]{4,}", text.lower()))


def load_aliases() -> dict:
    if ALIASES_FILE.exists():
        return json.loads(ALIASES_FILE.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    return {"concept_aliases": {}, "book_aliases": {}}


def load_all_concepts(aliases: dict) -> list[dict]:
    concepts: list[dict] = []
    for domain_dir in sorted(OUTPUT_DIR.iterdir()):
        if not domain_dir.is_dir():
            continue
        for chunk_file in sorted(domain_dir.glob("*.json")):
            if chunk_file.name.startswith("."):
                continue
            data = json.loads(chunk_file.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
            for c in data:
                c["concept"] = aliases["concept_aliases"].get(c["concept"], c["concept"])
                book = c.get("source", {}).get("book")
                if book:
                    c["source"]["book"] = aliases["book_aliases"].get(book, book)
                concepts.append(c)
    return concepts


def dedup_concepts(concepts: list[dict]) -> dict[str, dict]:
    """Fuzzy dedup: normalized concept name -> merged concept."""
    merged: dict[str, dict] = {}
    for c in concepts:
        key = slug(c["concept"])
        if key not in merged:
            merged[key] = c
            continue
        m = merged[key]
        if c.get("definition") and c["definition"] not in m.get("definition", ""):
            m["definition"] = (m.get("definition", "") + " " + c["definition"]).strip()
        if c.get("source", {}).get("book") and c["source"]["book"] != m.get("source", {}).get("book"):
            m.setdefault("other_sources", []).append(c["source"])
        for rel in c.get("relates_to", []):
            m.setdefault("relates_to", []).append(rel)
    return merged


def load_rules() -> list[dict]:
    specs = json.loads(SPECS_FILE.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    rules: list[dict] = []
    for profile, bundle in specs.items():
        for rule in bundle.get("rules", []):
            rule["_profile"] = profile
            rules.append(rule)
    return rules


def build_graph(concepts: dict[str, dict], rules: list[dict]) -> dict:
    nodes: list[dict] = []
    edges: list[dict] = []
    node_ids: set[str] = set()

    def add_node(node: dict) -> None:
        if node["id"] not in node_ids:
            nodes.append(node)
            node_ids.add(node["id"])

    def add_edge(source: str, target: str, etype: str, weight: float = 1.0) -> None:
        if source == target:
            return
        edges.append({"source": source, "target": target, "type": etype, "weight": weight})

    # ---- concept nodes + concept->concept edges + concept->book edges ----
    book_nodes: dict[str, dict] = {}
    for key, c in concepts.items():
        cid = f"concept:{key}"
        add_node({
            "id": cid,
            "type": "concept",
            "label": c["concept"],
            "definition": c.get("definition", ""),
            "domain": c.get("domain", "general"),
            "source": c.get("source", {}),
        })
        book = (c.get("source") or {}).get("book")
        if book:
            bid = f"book:{slug(book)}"
            if bid not in book_nodes:
                book_nodes[bid] = {"id": bid, "type": "book", "label": book, "kind": "book"}
                add_node(book_nodes[bid])
            add_edge(cid, bid, "cited_in")

        for rel in c.get("relates_to", []):
            target_name = rel.get("concept")
            if not target_name:
                continue
            target_name = rel.get("_alias") or target_name
            tid = f"concept:{slug(target_name)}"
            add_edge(cid, tid, rel.get("relation", "relates_to"), float(rel.get("weight", 0.5)))

    # ---- rule nodes + rule->concept / rule->book grounded_by edges ----
    for rule in rules:
        rid = f"rule:{rule['id']}"
        add_node({
            "id": rid,
            "type": "rule",
            "label": rule.get("title", ""),
            "severity": rule.get("severity", "info"),
            "profile": rule.get("_profile", ""),
            "rationale": rule.get("rationale", ""),
            "fix": rule.get("fix", ""),
        })

        rule_tokens = tokens(rule.get("title", "") + " " + rule.get("rationale", ""))
        grounded_any = False

        for ref in rule.get("references", []) or []:
            ref_concept = (ref.get("concept") or "").strip()
            ref_book = (ref.get("book") or ref.get("standard") or "").strip()
            ref_tokens = tokens(ref_concept + " " + ref_book)

            # rule -> concept (token overlap between reference and concept label)
            matched = False
            for key, c in concepts.items():
                if ref_tokens & tokens(c["concept"]):
                    add_edge(rid, f"concept:{key}", "grounded_by", 1.0)
                    matched = True
                    break
            if matched:
                grounded_any = True

            # rule -> book/standard node (always, for direct provenance)
            if ref_book:
                bid = f"book:{slug(ref_book)}"
                add_node({"id": bid, "type": "book", "label": ref_book,
                          "kind": "standard" if "standard" in ref else "book"})
                add_edge(rid, bid, "grounded_by", 1.0)
                grounded_any = True

        if not grounded_any:
            print(f"[WARN] rule {rule['id']} has zero grounded_by edges — check references")

    # ---- standards referenced by book_aliases stay kind:"book"; flag standards ----
    for n in nodes:
        if n["type"] == "book" and re.match(r"^(iec|iso|owasp|nfpa)\b", n["label"].lower()):
            n["kind"] = "standard"

    return {
        "version": "1.0",
        "generated_at": time.strftime("%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ", time.gmtime()),
        "stats": {
            "nodes": len(nodes),
            "edges": len(edges),
            "concepts": sum(1 for n in nodes if n["type"] == "concept"),
            "rules": sum(1 for n in nodes if n["type"] == "rule"),
            "books": sum(1 for n in nodes if n["type"] == "book"),
        },
        "nodes": nodes,
        "edges": edges,
    }


def generate_index(graph: dict) -> None:
    """Auto-generate docs/knowledge-index.md — the five-minute demo artifact."""
    rules_by_book: dict[str, set[str]] = {}
    concept_count_by_book: dict[str, int] = {}
    profile_by_book: dict[str, str] = {}

    for e in graph["edges"]:
        if e["type"] == "grounded_by":
            src, tgt = e["source"], e["target"]
            if src.startswith("rule:") and tgt.startswith("book:"):
                book = next((n["label"] for n in graph["nodes"] if n["id"] == tgt), tgt)
                rules_by_book.setdefault(book, set()).add(src.replace("rule:", ""))

    for n in graph["nodes"]:
        if n["type"] == "concept":
            book = (n.get("source") or {}).get("book")
            if book:
                concept_count_by_book[book] = concept_count_by_book.get(book, 0) + 1
                profile_by_book.setdefault(book, n.get("domain", "general"))

    lines = [
        "# Knowledge Index",
        "",
        "Auto-generated by `scripts/kg/assemble_graph.py` — do not edit by hand.",
        "Shows the reasoning chain: audit rule -> grounded in concept -> source book.",
        "",
        "| Book | Profile | Concepts | Grounded Rules |",
        "|---|---|---|---|",
    ]
    for book in sorted(set(concept_count_by_book) | set(rules_by_book)):
        profile = profile_by_book.get(book, "-")
        count = concept_count_by_book.get(book, 0)
        rules = ", ".join(sorted(rules_by_book.get(book, set()))) or "-"
        lines.append(f"| {book} | {profile} | {count} | {rules} |")

    INDEX_FILE.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    INDEX_FILE.write_text("\n".join(lines) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
    print(f"[OK] {INDEX_FILE}")


def main() -> None:
    if not SPECS_FILE.exists():
        raise SystemExit("[ERROR] specs.json missing — run scripts/build_knowledge.py first")

    aliases = load_aliases()
    concepts = dedup_concepts(load_all_concepts(aliases))
    rules = load_rules()

    if not concepts:
        raise SystemExit("[ERROR] No concepts extracted — run scripts/kg/extract.py first")

    graph = build_graph(concepts, rules)

    GRAPH_FILE.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    GRAPH_FILE.write_text(json.dumps(graph, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
    print(f"[OK] {GRAPH_FILE}")
    print(f"     {graph['stats']}")

    generate_index(graph)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
===== END FILE: scripts/kg/assemble_graph.py =====

===== FILE: scripts/kg/validate_graph.py =====
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
scripts/kg/validate_graph.py — integrity checks on public/knowledge/graph.json.
Exit code 1 on critical issues (dangling edges, duplicate ids), 0 otherwise.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import sys
from collections import Counter
from pathlib import Path

GRAPH_FILE = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2] / "public" / "knowledge" / "graph.json"


def main() -> None:
    if not GRAPH_FILE.exists():
        sys.exit("[ERROR] graph.json not found — run assemble_graph.py first")

    graph = json.loads(GRAPH_FILE.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    nodes = graph.get("nodes", [])
    edges = graph.get("edges", [])
    ids = [n["id"] for n in nodes]
    id_set = set(ids)

    critical = 0
    warnings = 0

    # duplicate ids
    dupes = [i for i, c in Counter(ids).items() if c > 1]
    if dupes:
        critical += 1
        print(f"[CRITICAL] duplicate node ids: {dupes[:5]}")

    # dangling edges
    dangling = [e for e in edges if e["source"] not in id_set or e["target"] not in id_set]
    if dangling:
        critical += 1
        print(f"[CRITICAL] {len(dangling)} dangling edges, e.g. {dangling[:3]}")

    # orphan nodes (no edges at all)
    touched: set[str] = set()
    for e in edges:
        touched.add(e["source"])
        touched.add(e["target"])
    orphans = [i for i in ids if i not in touched]
    if orphans:
        warnings += 1
        print(f"[WARN] {len(orphans)} orphan nodes (no edges): {orphans[:10]}")

    # concepts without a source book
    unsourced = [n["id"] for n in nodes
                 if n["type"] == "concept" and not (n.get("source") or {}).get("book")]
    if unsourced:
        warnings += 1
        print(f"[WARN] {len(unsourced)} concepts without source book: {unsourced[:10]}")

    # rules with zero grounded_by edges
    grounded = {e["source"] for e in edges if e["type"] == "grounded_by"}
    ungrounded = [n["id"] for n in nodes
                  if n["type"] == "rule" and n["id"] not in grounded]
    if ungrounded:
        warnings += 1
        print(f"[WARN] {len(ungrounded)} ungrounded rules: {ungrounded[:10]}")

    print(f"\n[REPORT] nodes={len(nodes)} edges={len(edges)} "
          f"critical={critical} warnings={warnings}")
    sys.exit(1 if critical else 0)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
===== END FILE: scripts/kg/validate_graph.py =====

===== FILE: scripts/kg/extract.py =====
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
scripts/kg/extract.py — Offline PDF -> structured-concept extraction.

Usage (from repo root):
  python scripts/kg/extract.py --source "/d/document/RepoScope/DataOps & Analytics Engineering" --domain dataops
  python scripts/kg/extract.py --source "<folder>" --domain iiot --limit-chunks 5   # smoke test

Requirements:
  pip install pymupdf
  Ollama running locally (ollama serve) with model pulled once:
    ollama pull qwen2.5:7b

Design notes:
- Fully offline: talks only to localhost Ollama. National outages cannot break it.
- Checkpointed: per-domain .state.json + one output file per chunk. Any crash
  or power loss resumes exactly where it stopped.
- format:"json" in the Ollama API constrains output to valid JSON
  (the main reason for locking the model choice to qwen2.5:7b).
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import hashlib
import json
import re
import sys
import time
import urllib.request
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Iterator

try:
    import fitz  # pymupdf
except ImportError:
    sys.exit("[ERROR] pymupdf missing. Run: pip install pymupdf")

KG_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parent

EXTRACTION_PROMPT = """Extract structured knowledge from the text below.
Return ONLY a JSON object matching this schema exactly:

{{
  "concepts": [
    {{
      "concept": "exact term from text",
      "definition": "one-sentence definition",
      "chapter": "chapter or section name if identifiable, else null",
      "page_range": "start-end if visible in the text, else null",
      "relates_to": [
        {{"concept": "related term", "relation": "enables|requires|contrasts|analogous_to", "weight": 0.0-1.0}}
      ]
    }}
  ]
}}

Rules:
- Extract only concepts explicitly defined or explained in the text.
- Maximum 5 concepts per chunk.
- If no clear concept exists, return {{"concepts": []}}.
- NEVER invent page numbers, chapter names, or relations.
- Relations must connect concepts actually present in the text.

TEXT:
{text}"""


def clean_book_title(filename: str) -> str:
    """Derive a readable book title from the PDF filename (strips vendor junk)."""
    name = Path(filename).stem
    junk = [" (z-lib.org)", " (z-lib.org).pdf", " - libgen.li", " (Z-Library)", "dokumen.pub_"]
    for j in junk:
        name = name.replace(j, "")
    name = re.sub(r"\s*\[[^\]]{10,}\]\s*", " ", name)     # long bracket blocks (ISBN/DOI junk)
    name = re.sub(r"\s+-\s+Anna'?s?\s+A\.?$", "", name, flags=re.IGNORECASE)
    name = re.sub(r"\s*\(\d{4}[^\)]*\)\s*$", "", name)     # trailing (year, publisher)
    return re.sub(r"\s{2,}", " ", name).strip(" -")


def chunk_pdf(pdf_path: Path, chunk_size: int = 3000) -> Iterator[dict]:
    """Split a PDF into ~chunk_size-char pieces on paragraph boundaries."""
    doc = fitz.open(pdf_path)
    buffer = ""
    start_page = 0
    try:
        for page_num, page in enumerate(doc):
            buffer += page.get_text("text") + "\n"
            while len(buffer) >= chunk_size:
                cut = buffer.rfind("\n", chunk_size // 2, chunk_size)
                if cut == -1:
                    cut = chunk_size
                piece, buffer = buffer[:cut], buffer[cut:]
                yield _make_chunk(pdf_path, start_page, page_num, piece)
                start_page = page_num
        if buffer.strip():
            yield _make_chunk(pdf_path, start_page, len(doc) - 1, buffer)
    finally:
        doc.close()


def _make_chunk(pdf_path: Path, start_page: int, end_page: int, text: str) -> dict:
    chunk_id = hashlib.md5(f"{pdf_path.name}:{start_page}:{end_page}".encode()).hexdigest()[:10]
    return {
        "id": chunk_id,
        "source": pdf_path.name,
        "book": clean_book_title(pdf_path.name),
        "start_page": start_page + 1,
        "end_page": end_page + 1,
        "text": text,
    }


def call_ollama(prompt: str, model: str, host: str, retries: int = 2) -> str:
    """POST to the local Ollama API with JSON-constrained output, temperature 0."""
    payload = json.dumps({
        "model": model,
        "prompt": prompt,
        "format": "json",
        "stream": False,
        "options": {"temperature": 0, "num_predict": 1024},
    }).encode("utf-8")

    last_err: Exception | None = None
    for attempt in range(retries + 1):
        try:
            req = urllib.request.Request(
                f"{host}/api/generate",
                data=payload,
                headers={"Content-Type": "application/json"},
                method="POST",
            )
            with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=300) as resp:
                data = json.loads(resp.read().decode("utf-8"))
            return str(data.get("response", ""))
        except Exception as e:  # transient Ollama hiccups — retry, then give up on this chunk
            last_err = e
            time.sleep(2 * (attempt + 1))
    raise RuntimeError(f"Ollama failed after {retries + 1} attempts: {last_err}")


def extract_concepts(chunk: dict, model: str, host: str, domain: str) -> list[dict]:
    raw = call_ollama(
        EXTRACTION_PROMPT.format(text=chunk["text"][:2500]), model, host
    )

    start, end = raw.find("{"), raw.rfind("}") + 1
    if start == -1 or end == 0:
        return []

    try:
        parsed = json.loads(raw[start:end])
    except json.JSONDecodeError:
        return []

    concepts = parsed.get("concepts", []) if isinstance(parsed, dict) else []
    cleaned: list[dict] = []
    for c in concepts[:5]:
        if not isinstance(c, dict) or not c.get("concept"):
            continue
        cleaned.append({
            "concept": str(c["concept"]).strip(),
            "definition": str(c.get("definition") or "").strip(),
            "source": {
                "book": chunk["book"],
                "chapter": c.get("chapter"),
                "page_range": c.get("page_range") or f"{chunk['start_page']}-{chunk['end_page']}",
            },
            "domain": domain,
            "relates_to": c.get("relates_to") or [],
            "rule_refs": [],
            "chunk_id": chunk["id"],
            "extracted_at": time.strftime("%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ", time.gmtime()),
            "model_used": model,
        })
    return cleaned


def main() -> None:
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Offline PDF -> concept extraction (Ollama, checkpointed)")
    ap.add_argument("--source", required=True, help="Folder containing domain PDFs")
    ap.add_argument("--domain", required=True, choices=["dataops", "iiot", "webapp", "ml"])
    ap.add_argument("--model", default="qwen2.5:7b")
    ap.add_argument("--host", default="http://localhost:11434")
    ap.add_argument("--chunk-size", type=int, default=3000)
    ap.add_argument("--limit-chunks", type=int, default=0, help="0 = no limit (smoke tests: use 1-5)")
    ap.add_argument("--delay", type=float, default=0.0, help="Seconds between chunks (laptop cooling)")
    args = ap.parse_args()

    source_dir = Path(args.source)
    if not source_dir.is_dir():
        sys.exit(f"[ERROR] Source folder not found: {source_dir}")

    output_dir = KG_DIR / "output" / args.domain
    output_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    state_file = output_dir / ".state.json"

    state = {"completed_chunks": [], "failed_chunks": []}
    if state_file.exists():
        state = json.loads(state_file.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))

    pdfs = sorted(source_dir.glob("*.pdf"))
    if not pdfs:
        sys.exit(f"[ERROR] No PDFs found in {source_dir}")

    print(f"[KG] {len(pdfs)} PDFs | domain={args.domain} | model={args.model} | "
          f"resume={len(state['completed_chunks'])} done, {len(state['failed_chunks'])} failed")

    processed = 0
    for pdf_path in pdfs:
        print(f"\n[PDF] {pdf_path.name}")
        for chunk in chunk_pdf(pdf_path, args.chunk_size):
            if chunk["id"] in state["completed_chunks"]:
                print(f"  [SKIP] {chunk['id']} (already done)")
                continue

            try:
                concepts = extract_concepts(chunk, args.model, args.host, args.domain)
                out_file = output_dir / f"{chunk['id']}.json"
                out_file.write_text(
                    json.dumps(concepts, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding="utf-8"
                )
                state["completed_chunks"].append(chunk["id"])
                state_file.write_text(json.dumps(state, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
                processed += 1
                print(f"  [OK]   {chunk['id']} p{chunk['start_page']}-{chunk['end_page']}: "
                      f"{len(concepts)} concepts")

                if args.delay > 0:
                    time.sleep(args.delay)
                if args.limit_chunks and processed >= args.limit_chunks:
                    print("\n[STOP] --limit-chunks reached (smoke test mode)")
                    return

            except Exception as e:
                print(f"  [FAIL] {chunk['id']}: {e}")
                state["failed_chunks"].append(chunk["id"])
                state_file.write_text(json.dumps(state, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
                # continue — failed chunks are skipped and reviewable later

    print(f"\n[DONE] run complete. chunks ok={len(state['completed_chunks'])} "
          f"failed={len(state['failed_chunks'])} this-run={processed}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
===== END FILE: scripts/kg/extract.py =====

===== FILE: components/Board.tsx =====
'use client';

import type { FindingSeverity } from '@/lib/types';

/**
 * A section header + summary the Board renders as a card.
 * This matches the fields actually consumed below — `title`, `status`, `detail`.
 * If you later migrate Board to render a per-section findings list,
 * extend this interface with `findings: Finding[]` and update the JSX.
 */
export interface BoardSection {
  title: string;
  status: 'pass' | 'warn' | 'fail';
  detail?: string;
}

interface BoardProps {
  sections: BoardSection[];
}

/** Maps a Board status onto the shared FindingSeverity vocabulary. */
const STATUS_TO_SEVERITY: Record<BoardSection['status'], FindingSeverity> = {
  pass: 'info',
  warn: 'warning',
  fail: 'error',
};

export default function Board({ sections }: BoardProps) {
  const getStatusColor = (status: BoardSection['status']): string => {
    switch (status) {
      case 'pass': return 'pill-pass';
      case 'warn': return 'pill-warn';
      case 'fail': return 'pill-fail';
      default:     return 'pill-info';
    }
  };

  const getIcon = (status: BoardSection['status']): string => {
    switch (status) {
      case 'pass': return 'PASS';
      case 'warn': return 'WARN';
      case 'fail': return 'FAIL';
      default:     return 'INFO';
    }
  };

  if (!sections || sections.length === 0) {
    return (
      <div className="card-static rounded-2xl p-6">
        <p className="text-slate-400 text-sm">No sections to display</p>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="card-static rounded-2xl p-6">
      <h3 className="text-lg font-black text-white mb-4">Repository Sections</h3>
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-3">
        {sections.map((section, idx) => {
          // Kept so future code can map a Board status onto severity without
          // duplicating the switch above. Reserved for the "findings list" refactor.
          void STATUS_TO_SEVERITY[section.status];

          return (
            <div key={idx} className="find-row">
              <div className={`sev-ico sev-${section.status}`}>
                {getIcon(section.status)}
              </div>
              <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-1">
                  <span className="font-bold text-white text-sm truncate">
                    {section.title}
                  </span>
                  <span className={`pill ${getStatusColor(section.status)}`}>
                    {section.status.toUpperCase()}
                  </span>
                </div>
                <p className="text-slate-400 text-xs line-clamp-2">
                  {section.detail || 'No details available'}
                </p>
              </div>
            </div>
          );
        })}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
===== END FILE: components/Board.tsx =====

===== FILE: components/ScoreBoard.tsx =====
'use client';

import type { AuditScore, FindingSeverity } from '@/lib/types';

interface ScoreBoardProps {
  /** Overall 0-100 score. Duplicated inside `explanation.score` for the caller's convenience. */
  score: number;
  /** Return value of `scoreAndExplain()` from lib/audit.ts. */
  explanation: AuditScore;
}

const SEVERITY_ICO: Record<FindingSeverity, string> = {
  critical: '!!!',
  error: '!!',
  warning: '!',
  info: 'i',
};

const SEVERITY_CLASS: Record<FindingSeverity, string> = {
  critical: 'sev-error',
  error: 'sev-error',
  warning: 'sev-warning',
  info: 'sev-info',
};

export default function ScoreBoard({ score, explanation }: ScoreBoardProps) {
  const C = 2 * Math.PI * 36;
  const color = score >= 80 ? '#34d399' : score >= 60 ? '#fbbf24' : '#fb7185';
  const label = score >= 80 ? 'Excellent' : score >= 60 ? 'Needs attention' : 'Critical';

  const offset = C - (score / 100) * C;

  return (
    <div className="card-static rounded-2xl p-6 anim">
      <div className="flex items-center gap-8 mb-6">
        <div className="relative w-24 h-24 flex-shrink-0">
          <svg className="w-24 h-24 -rotate-90">
            <circle
              cx="48"
              cy="48"
              r="36"
              stroke="#22314e"
              strokeWidth="8"
              fill="none"
            />
            <circle
              cx="48"
              cy="48"
              r="36"
              stroke={color}
              strokeWidth="8"
              fill="none"
              strokeDasharray={C}
              strokeDashoffset={offset}
              strokeLinecap="round"
              style={{ transition: 'stroke-dashoffset 1s ease' }}
            />
          </svg>
          <div className="absolute inset-0 flex items-center justify-center">
            <span className="text-3xl font-black" style={{ color }}>
              {score}
            </span>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="flex-1">
          <div className="mb-2">
            <span className="text-lg font-black text-white block">Health Score</span>
            <span className="text-xs font-extrabold" style={{ color }}>{label}</span>
          </div>
          <div className="text-[11px] font-mono text-slate-400 mt-1">
            {explanation.critical > 0 && <span>{explanation.critical} critical </span>}
            {explanation.errors > 0 && <span>{explanation.errors} errors </span>}
            {explanation.warnings > 0 && <span>{explanation.warnings} warnings </span>}
            {explanation.infos > 0 && <span>{explanation.infos} info</span>}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="border-t border-edge pt-4">
        <h3 className="text-sm font-black text-white mb-3">Why this score?</h3>
        <div className="space-y-2">
          {explanation.items.map((item, idx) => {
            const sevClass = SEVERITY_CLASS[item.severity];
            const ico = SEVERITY_ICO[item.severity];

            return (
              <div key={idx} className="find-row">
                <div className={`sev-ico ${sevClass}`}>{ico}</div>

                <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                  <div className="mb-1">
                    <span className="font-bold text-white text-sm">
                      {item.title}
                    </span>
                    {item.points > 0 && (
                      <span className="ml-3 text-xs font-mono text-rosex font-bold">
                        (-{item.points} pts)
                      </span>
                    )}
                    <span className="ml-2 text-[10px] font-mono text-slate-500">
                      {item.ruleId}
                    </span>
                  </div>

                  <p className="text-slate-400 text-xs mb-1">{item.detail}</p>

                  <p className="text-mint text-xs">
                    <span className="font-bold">Fix:</span> {item.fix}
                  </p>
                </div>
              </div>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
